\displaystyle\,=\,\left.\text{\footnotesize{$\begin{pmatrix}\text{\footnotesize{$\begin{pmatrix}{\text{1}}\hskip-0.56905pt_{3}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}-\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}\big({{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\star}}-{{\boldsymbol{q}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\star}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{p}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\star}}-({\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\star}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{q}}}){\raisebox{0.51663pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}({\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\star}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{p}}})\big)\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}&\tfrac{q^{2}}{{\ell}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}{\raisebox{0.51663pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\\[3.0pt]
-\tfrac{p^{2}}{{\ell}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}{\raisebox{0.51663pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}&{\text{1}}\hskip-0.56905pt_{3}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}-\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}\big({{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\star}}+{{\boldsymbol{p}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\star}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{q}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\star}}+({\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\star}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{p}}}){\raisebox{0.51663pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}({\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\star}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{q}}})\big)\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\end{pmatrix}$}}&\text{\footnotesize{$\begin{pmatrix}{{\boldsymbol{0}}}&{{\boldsymbol{0}}}\\[3.0pt]
{{\boldsymbol{0}}}&{{\boldsymbol{0}}}\end{pmatrix}$}}\\[18.0pt]
\text{\footnotesize{$\begin{pmatrix}\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}\big(\tfrac{2{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}}(1-\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau})+\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}w\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\big){{\boldsymbol{p}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\star}}&-\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}\big(\tfrac{2{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}}(1-\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau})+\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}w\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\big){{\boldsymbol{q}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\star}}\\[3.0pt]
(u+\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}})\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,{{\boldsymbol{p}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\star}}&-(u+\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}})\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,{{\boldsymbol{q}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\star}}\end{pmatrix}$}}&\text{\footnotesize{$\begin{pmatrix}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}&\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\\[3.0pt]
-{\ell}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}&\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\end{pmatrix}$}}\end{pmatrix}$}}\right|_{{{\boldsymbol{x}}}={{\boldsymbol{x}}}_{0}}

(456)

\displaystyle\tfrac{\uppartial{{\boldsymbol{x}}}_{0}}{\uppartial{{\boldsymbol{x}}}_{\tau}}

\displaystyle\,=\,\left.\text{\footnotesize{$\begin{pmatrix}\text{\footnotesize{$\begin{pmatrix}{\text{1}}\hskip-0.56905pt_{3}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}+\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}\big({{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\star}}-{{\boldsymbol{q}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\star}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{p}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\star}}-({\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\star}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{q}}}){\raisebox{0.51663pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}({\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\star}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{p}}})\big)\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}&-\tfrac{q^{2}}{{\ell}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}{\raisebox{0.51663pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\\[3.0pt]
\tfrac{p^{2}}{{\ell}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}{\raisebox{0.51663pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}&{\text{1}}\hskip-0.56905pt_{3}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}+\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}\big({{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\star}}+{{\boldsymbol{p}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\star}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{q}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\star}}+({\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\star}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{p}}}){\raisebox{0.51663pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}({\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\star}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{q}}})\big)\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\end{pmatrix}$}}&\text{\footnotesize{$\begin{pmatrix}{{\boldsymbol{0}}}&{{\boldsymbol{0}}}\\[3.0pt]
{{\boldsymbol{0}}}&{{\boldsymbol{0}}}\end{pmatrix}$}}\\[18.0pt]
\text{\footnotesize{$\begin{pmatrix}\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}\big(\tfrac{2{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}}(1-\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau})-\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}w\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\big){{\boldsymbol{p}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\star}}&-\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}\big(\tfrac{2{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}}(1-\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau})-\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}w\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\big){{\boldsymbol{q}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\star}}\\[3.0pt]
-(u+\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}})\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,{{\boldsymbol{p}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\star}}&(u+\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}})\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,{{\boldsymbol{q}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\star}}\end{pmatrix}$}}&\text{\footnotesize{$\begin{pmatrix}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}&-\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\\[3.0pt]
{\ell}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}&\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\end{pmatrix}$}}\end{pmatrix}$}}\right|_{{{\boldsymbol{x}}}={{\boldsymbol{x}}}_{\tau}}

The above follow from the unsimplified Kepler flow in Eq.(
B.1
).
If we now use the integrals of motion
q=1
and and
\lambda={\hat{{{\boldsymbol{q}}}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{p}}}=0
— implying
{\ell}\simeq p
and the relations in Eq.(
A
)-Eq.(
A
) of appendix
A
— to simplify the above, we obtain:

\displaystyle\tfrac{\uppartial{{\boldsymbol{x}}}_{\tau}}{\uppartial{{\boldsymbol{x}}}_{0}}

\displaystyle\,\simeq\,\left.\text{\footnotesize{$\begin{pmatrix}\text{\footnotesize{$\begin{pmatrix}{\text{1}}\hskip-0.56905pt_{3}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}-{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\star}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}&\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\star}}{\raisebox{0.51663pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\star}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\\[3.0pt]
-{\ell}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\star}}{\raisebox{0.51663pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\star}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}&{\text{1}}\hskip-0.56905pt_{3}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}-{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\star}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\end{pmatrix}$}}&\text{\footnotesize{$\begin{pmatrix}{{\boldsymbol{0}}}&{{\boldsymbol{0}}}\\[3.0pt]
{{\boldsymbol{0}}}&{{\boldsymbol{0}}}\end{pmatrix}$}}\\[18.0pt]
\text{\footnotesize{$\begin{pmatrix}-\big(\tfrac{2{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}}(1-\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau})+\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}w\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\big){{\boldsymbol{q}}}^{{\scriptscriptstyle{\textsf{{T}}}}}&-\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}\big(\tfrac{2{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}}(1-\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau})+\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}w\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\big){\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}^{{\scriptscriptstyle{\textsf{{T}}}}}\\[3.0pt]
-{\ell}(u+\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}})\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,{{\boldsymbol{q}}}^{{\scriptscriptstyle{\textsf{{T}}}}}&-(u+\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}})\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}^{{\scriptscriptstyle{\textsf{{T}}}}}\end{pmatrix}$}}&\text{\footnotesize{$\begin{pmatrix}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}&\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\\[3.0pt]
-{\ell}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}&\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\end{pmatrix}$}}\end{pmatrix}$}}\right|_{{{\boldsymbol{x}}}={{\boldsymbol{x}}}_{0}}

(457)

\displaystyle\tfrac{\uppartial{{\boldsymbol{x}}}_{0}}{\uppartial{{\boldsymbol{x}}}_{\tau}}

\displaystyle\,\simeq\,\left.\text{\footnotesize{$\begin{pmatrix}\text{\footnotesize{$\begin{pmatrix}{\text{1}}\hskip-0.56905pt_{3}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}+{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\star}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}&-\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}{\raisebox{0.51663pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\\[3.0pt]
{\ell}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}{\raisebox{0.51663pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}&{\text{1}}\hskip-0.56905pt_{3}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}+{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\star}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\end{pmatrix}$}}&\text{\footnotesize{$\begin{pmatrix}{{\boldsymbol{0}}}&{{\boldsymbol{0}}}\\[3.0pt]
{{\boldsymbol{0}}}&{{\boldsymbol{0}}}\end{pmatrix}$}}\\[18.0pt]
\text{\footnotesize{$\begin{pmatrix}-\big(\tfrac{2{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}}(1-\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau})-\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}w\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\big){{\boldsymbol{q}}}^{{\scriptscriptstyle{\textsf{{T}}}}}&-\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}\big(\tfrac{2{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}}(1-\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau})-\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}w\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\big){\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}^{{\scriptscriptstyle{\textsf{{T}}}}}\\[3.0pt]
{\ell}(u+\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}})\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,{{\boldsymbol{q}}}^{{\scriptscriptstyle{\textsf{{T}}}}}&(u+\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}})\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}^{{\scriptscriptstyle{\textsf{{T}}}}}\end{pmatrix}$}}&\text{\footnotesize{$\begin{pmatrix}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}&-\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\\[3.0pt]
{\ell}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}&\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\end{pmatrix}$}}\end{pmatrix}$}}\right|_{{{\boldsymbol{x}}}={{\boldsymbol{x}}}_{\tau}}

In all the above, for the upper left sub-matrices we have used the relation:

\displaystyle{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}{\raisebox{0.58122pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}\;=\;{\text{1}}\hskip-0.56905pt_{3}-{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}{\raisebox{0.58122pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}-({\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\star}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}){\raisebox{0.58122pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}({\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\star}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}})\;=\;{\text{1}}\hskip-0.56905pt_{3}-{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{q}}}}}{\raisebox{0.58122pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{q}}}}}-({\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\star}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{q}}}}}){\raisebox{0.58122pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}({\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\star}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{q}}}}})\;\simeq\;{\text{1}}\hskip-0.56905pt_{3}-{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{q}}}}}{\raisebox{0.58122pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{q}}}}}-{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}{\raisebox{0.58122pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}

(458)

B.2  Simplified Matrix Equations and STM

The above matrix ODEs are without simplifying anything using the integrals of motion
q=1
and
\lambda={\hat{{{\boldsymbol{q}}}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{p}}}=0
(and thus
{\ell}^{2}\simeq p^{2}
).
Although not required, we will now do so for simplicity.
The Kepler dynamics in Eq.(
1.4
) then simplify to:

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{cc}{\text{\scriptsize{Kepler-type }}}\\
{\text{\scriptsize{dynamics}}}\end{array}

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{llllll}\acute{{{\boldsymbol{q}}}}\,\simeq\,{{\boldsymbol{p}}}&,\hskip 9.24994pt\acute{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}\,\simeq\,-p^{2}{{\boldsymbol{q}}}\,\simeq\,-{\ell}^{2}{{\boldsymbol{q}}}\\[4.0pt]
\acute{u}\,=\,w&,\hskip 9.24994pt\acute{w}=-{\ell}^{2}u+{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}\end{array}\hskip 18.49988pt\hskip 9.24994pt{\text{\scriptsize{or,}}}\hskip 18.49988pt\hskip 9.24994pt\begin{array}[]{llllll}\mathring{{{\boldsymbol{q}}}}\,\simeq\,\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}{{\boldsymbol{p}}}\,\simeq\,{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}&,\hskip 9.24994pt\mathring{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}\,\simeq\,-{\ell}{{\boldsymbol{q}}}\\[4.0pt]
\mathring{u}\,=\,w/{\ell}&,\hskip 9.24994pt\mathring{w}=-{\ell}u+{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}/{{\ell}}\end{array}

where
{\ell}={\ell}_{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}\simeq p_{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}=p
is preserved. The above is equivalent to the following linear ODE for
{{\boldsymbol{x}}}:=({{\boldsymbol{q}}},{{\boldsymbol{p}}},u,w)
,

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{{\boldsymbol{x}}}=({{\boldsymbol{q}}},{{\boldsymbol{p}}},u,w)

\displaystyle\boxed{\begin{array}[]{rlll}&\acute{{{\boldsymbol{x}}}}\,\simeq\,\tilde{\Lambda}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{x}}}\,+\,{{\boldsymbol{k}}}\\[5.0pt]
{\text{\footnotesize{or,}}}&\mathring{{{\boldsymbol{x}}}}\,\simeq\,\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}\tilde{\Lambda}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{x}}}\,+\,\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}{{\boldsymbol{k}}}\end{array}\hskip 9.24994pt\Rightarrow\hskip 18.49988pt{{\boldsymbol{x}}}_{\tau}\simeq\Phi_{\tau}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{x}}}_{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}\,+\,{{\boldsymbol{{\sigma}}}}_{\tau}}

\displaystyle\tau={\ell}s\hskip 9.24994pt

with
{{\boldsymbol{k}}}=(0,0,\dots,{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1})\in{\mathbb{R}}^{8}
and
{{\boldsymbol{{\sigma}}}}_{\tau}
the same as in Eq.(
B.1
), and
where
\tilde{\Lambda}=\tilde{\Lambda}_{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}
is a matrix of integrals of motion given as follows, along with
\Phi_{\tau}
:

\displaystyle\tilde{\Lambda}:=\text{\footnotesize{$\begin{pmatrix}\Lambda_{6}&0\\
0&\Lambda_{2}\end{pmatrix}$}}\in\mathfrak{sp}^{6;2}_{\scriptscriptstyle{{\mathbb{R}}}}\hskip 18.00003pt,\hskip 18.00003pt\Phi_{\tau}:={\mathrm{e}}^{\frac{1}{{\ell}}\tilde{\Lambda}\tau}={\mathrm{e}}^{\tilde{\Lambda}s}=\text{\footnotesize{$\begin{pmatrix}{\mathrm{e}}^{\frac{1}{{\ell}}\Lambda_{6}\tau}&0\\
0&{\mathrm{e}}^{\frac{1}{{\ell}}\Lambda_{2}\tau}\end{pmatrix}$}}\in\mathrm{Sp}^{6;2}_{\scriptscriptstyle{{\mathbb{R}}}}\hskip 18.00003pt,\hskip 18.00003pt\begin{array}[]{rllll}{{\boldsymbol{k}}}&\!\!\!=\,{\text{\footnotesize{Eq.\eqref{KepMats_nonsimp}}}}\\[3.0pt]
{{\boldsymbol{{\sigma}}}}_{\tau}&\!\!\!=\,{\text{\footnotesize{Eq.\eqref{KepMats_nonsimp}}}}\\[3.0pt]
{{\boldsymbol{{\varsigma}}}}_{\tau}&\!\!\!:=-\Phi_{\tau}^{\scriptscriptstyle{-\!1}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{{\sigma}}}}_{\tau}={{\boldsymbol{{\sigma}}}}_{-\tau}\end{array}

where, for any dimension
n
,
\Lambda_{2{{\textit{{{n}}}}}}\in\mathfrak{sp}^{2{{\textit{{{n}}}}}}_{\scriptscriptstyle{{\mathbb{R}}}}
and
{\mathrm{e}}^{\Lambda_{2{{\textit{{{n}}}}}}\tau/{\ell}}\in\mathrm{Sp}^{2{{\textit{{{n}}}}}}_{\scriptscriptstyle{{\mathbb{R}}}}
have the form implied by Eq.(
B.1
) or Eq.(
139
)
(see footnote
40
40

40

Specifically, for any finite dimension
n
:

\displaystyle\Lambda_{2{{\textit{{{n}}}}}}:=\text{\footnotesize{$\begin{pmatrix}0&{\text{1}}\hskip-0.56905pt_{{\textit{{{n}}}}}\\
-{\ell}^{2}{\text{1}}\hskip-0.56905pt_{{\textit{{{n}}}}}&0\end{pmatrix}$}}\in\mathfrak{sp}^{2{{\textit{{{n}}}}}}_{\scriptscriptstyle{{\mathbb{R}}}}\qquad,\qquad{\mathrm{e}}^{\Lambda_{2{{\textit{{{n}}}}}}s}={\mathrm{e}}^{\Lambda_{2{{\textit{{{n}}}}}}\tau/{\ell}}=\text{\footnotesize{$\begin{pmatrix}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,{\text{1}}\hskip-0.56905pt_{{\textit{{{n}}}}}&\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,{\text{1}}\hskip-0.56905pt_{{\textit{{{n}}}}}\\
-{\ell}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,{\text{1}}\hskip-0.56905pt_{{\textit{{{n}}}}}&\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,{\text{1}}\hskip-0.56905pt_{{\textit{{{n}}}}}\end{pmatrix}$}}\in\mathrm{Sp}^{2{{\textit{{{n}}}}}}_{\scriptscriptstyle{{\mathbb{R}}}}

(470)

).
The solution is easily inverted using
\Phi_{\tau}^{\scriptscriptstyle{-\!1}}=\Phi_{-\tau}
:

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\begin{array}[]{ccccc}{{\boldsymbol{x}}}_{\tau}\simeq\phi_{\tau}({{\boldsymbol{x}}}_{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}})=\Phi_{\tau}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{x}}}_{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}+{{\boldsymbol{{\sigma}}}}_{\tau}\hskip 18.49988pt\leftrightarrow\hskip 18.49988pt{{\boldsymbol{x}}}_{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}\simeq\phi_{\tau}^{\scriptscriptstyle{-\!1}}({{\boldsymbol{x}}}_{\tau})=\Phi_{\tau}^{\scriptscriptstyle{-\!1}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{x}}}_{\tau}+{{\boldsymbol{{\varsigma}}}}_{\tau}\\[10.0pt]
\begin{array}[]{lllllll}{{\boldsymbol{q}}}_{\tau}\,\simeq\,{{\boldsymbol{q}}}_{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}+\tfrac{1}{{\ell}_{0}}{{\boldsymbol{p}}}_{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\\[4.0pt]
{{\boldsymbol{p}}}_{\tau}\,\simeq\,{{\boldsymbol{p}}}_{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}-{\ell}_{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}{{\boldsymbol{q}}}_{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\\[4.0pt]
u_{\tau}\,=\,u_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}+\tfrac{1}{{\ell}_{0}}w_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}+\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}_{0}^{2}}(1-\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau})\\[4.0pt]
w_{\tau}\,=\,w_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}-{\ell}_{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}u_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}+\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}_{0}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\end{array}\hskip 18.49988pt\leftrightarrow\hskip 18.49988pt\begin{array}[]{lll}{{\boldsymbol{q}}}_{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}\,\simeq\,{{\boldsymbol{q}}}_{\tau}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}-\tfrac{1}{{\ell}_{\tau}}{{\boldsymbol{p}}}_{\tau}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\\[4.0pt]
{{\boldsymbol{p}}}_{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}\,\simeq\,{{\boldsymbol{p}}}_{\tau}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}+{\ell}_{\tau}{{\boldsymbol{q}}}_{\tau}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\\[4.0pt]
u_{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}\,=\,u_{\tau}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}-\tfrac{1}{{\ell}_{\tau}}w_{\tau}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}+\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}_{\tau}^{2}}(1-\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau})\\[4.0pt]
w_{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}\,=\,w_{\tau}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}+{\ell}_{\tau}u_{\tau}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}-\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}_{\tau}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\end{array}\end{array}

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{lll}{\ell}_{\tau}={\ell}_{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}\\[2.0pt]
{\ell}\simeq p\end{array}

We have defined
\phi_{\tau}
as the Kepler flow in coordinates
{{\boldsymbol{x}}}
that has been simplified with
q=1
and
\lambda={\hat{{{\boldsymbol{q}}}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{p}}}=0
.
Note
\phi_{\tau}^{\scriptscriptstyle{-\!1}}=\phi_{-\tau}
.

•

Angular momentum dependence.
The comments around Eq.(
453
)-Eq.(
454
) regarding angular momentum dependence apply the same to all of the above simplified relations. Yet, with the simplified relations, this dependence is only on the angular momentum
magnitude
,
{\ell}
, which simplifies to
{\ell}\simeq p
.
That is,
\tilde{\Lambda}=\tilde{\Lambda}({\ell})
,
\Phi_{\tau}=\Phi_{\tau}({\ell})
,
{{\boldsymbol{{\sigma}}}}_{\tau}={{\boldsymbol{{\sigma}}}}_{\tau}({\ell})
, etc., with
{\ell}\simeq p
.
To clarify, we could write the solutions in Eq.(
B.2
) as:

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\begin{array}[]{ccccc}\begin{array}[]{lllllll}{{\boldsymbol{q}}}_{\tau}\,\simeq\,{{\boldsymbol{q}}}_{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}+{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}_{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\\[4.0pt]
{{\boldsymbol{p}}}_{\tau}\,\simeq\,{{\boldsymbol{p}}}_{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}-p_{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}{{\boldsymbol{q}}}_{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\\[4.0pt]
u_{\tau}\,\simeq\,u_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}+\tfrac{1}{p_{0}}w_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}+\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{p_{0}^{2}}(1-\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau})\\[4.0pt]
w_{\tau}\,\simeq\,w_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}-p_{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}u_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}+\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{p_{0}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\end{array}\hskip 18.49988pt\leftrightarrow\hskip 18.49988pt\begin{array}[]{lll}{{\boldsymbol{q}}}_{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}\,\simeq\,{{\boldsymbol{q}}}_{\tau}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}-{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}_{\tau}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\\[4.0pt]
{{\boldsymbol{p}}}_{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}\,\simeq\,{{\boldsymbol{p}}}_{\tau}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}+p_{\tau}{{\boldsymbol{q}}}_{\tau}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\\[4.0pt]
u_{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}\,\simeq\,u_{\tau}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}-\tfrac{1}{p_{\tau}}w_{\tau}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}+\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{p_{\tau}^{2}}(1-\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau})\\[4.0pt]
w_{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}\,\simeq\,w_{\tau}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}+p_{\tau}u_{\tau}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}-\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{p_{\tau}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\end{array}\end{array}

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{lll}p_{\tau}=p_{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}\\[3.0pt]
{{\boldsymbol{p}}}_{\tau}\neq{{\boldsymbol{p}}}_{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}\end{array}

•

Back to “standard” ordering.
We have been using the modified coordinate ordering
{{\boldsymbol{x}}}=({{\boldsymbol{q}}},{{\boldsymbol{p}}},u,w)
. If we return to the standard configuration-momentum coordinate ordering,
{{\boldsymbol{x}}}=({{\boldsymbol{q}}},u,{{\boldsymbol{p}}},w)
, then the above simplified matrix ODE takes the form

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt{{\boldsymbol{x}}}=({{\boldsymbol{q}}},u,{{\boldsymbol{p}}},w)

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{rlll}&\acute{{{\boldsymbol{x}}}}\,\simeq\,\Lambda_{8}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{x}}}\,+\,{{\boldsymbol{k}}}\\[5.0pt]
{\text{\footnotesize{or,}}}&\mathring{{{\boldsymbol{x}}}}\,\simeq\,\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}\Lambda_{8}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{x}}}\,+\,\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}{{\boldsymbol{k}}}\end{array}\hskip 18.49988pt\Rightarrow\hskip 18.49988pt{{\boldsymbol{x}}}_{\tau}\simeq\Phi_{\tau}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{x}}}_{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}\,+\,{{\boldsymbol{{\sigma}}}}_{\tau}

where everything is the “same”, up to a re-ordering, of what was given in Eq.(
B.2
)-Eq.(
B.2
):

\displaystyle\Lambda_{8}:=\text{\footnotesize{$\begin{pmatrix}0&{\text{1}}\hskip-0.56905pt_{4}\\
-{\ell}^{2}{\text{1}}\hskip-0.56905pt_{4}&0\end{pmatrix}$}}\in\mathfrak{sp}^{8}_{\scriptscriptstyle{{\mathbb{R}}}}

,

\displaystyle\Phi_{\tau}={\mathrm{e}}^{\frac{1}{{\ell}}\Lambda_{8}\tau}=\text{\footnotesize{$\begin{pmatrix}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,{\text{1}}\hskip-0.56905pt_{4}&\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,{\text{1}}\hskip-0.56905pt_{4}\\
-{\ell}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,{\text{1}}\hskip-0.56905pt_{4}&\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,{\text{1}}\hskip-0.56905pt_{4}\end{pmatrix}$}}\in\mathrm{Sp}^{8}_{\scriptscriptstyle{{\mathbb{R}}}}

,

\displaystyle{{\boldsymbol{{\sigma}}}}_{\tau}=\text{\footnotesize{$\begin{pmatrix}{{\boldsymbol{0}}}\\
\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}}(1-\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau})\\
{{\boldsymbol{0}}}\\
\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\end{pmatrix}$}}

,

\displaystyle{{\boldsymbol{{\varsigma}}}}_{\tau}={{\boldsymbol{{\sigma}}}}_{-\tau}

(494)

where
\Lambda
has eigenvalues
\pm{\mathrm{i}}{\ell}
(four of each sign). Note we have re-used some notation (
{{\boldsymbol{x}}}
,
\Phi_{\tau}
,
{{\boldsymbol{{\sigma}}}}_{\tau}
, and
{{\boldsymbol{{\varsigma}}}}_{\tau}
) but now with a slightly different meaning (just re-ordering of indices).

Kepler STM from Simplified Solutions.

We continue to use the ordering
{{\boldsymbol{x}}}=({{\boldsymbol{q}}},{{\boldsymbol{p}}},u,w)
. The (simplified) matrix
\tfrac{\uppartial{{\boldsymbol{x}}}_{\tau}}{\uppartial{{\boldsymbol{x}}}_{0}}
is found from the simplified solutions in Eq.(

) or Eq.(

). Taking into account the dependence on
{\ell}\simeq p
, this leads to:

\displaystyle\tfrac{\uppartial{{\boldsymbol{x}}}_{\tau}}{\uppartial{{\boldsymbol{x}}}_{0}}

\displaystyle\,\simeq\,\left.\text{\footnotesize{$\begin{pmatrix}\text{\footnotesize{$\begin{pmatrix}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,{\text{1}}\hskip-0.56905pt_{3}&\tfrac{1}{p}({\text{1}}\hskip-0.56905pt_{3}-{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}{\raisebox{0.51663pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}})\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\\[3.0pt]
-p\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,{\text{1}}\hskip-0.56905pt_{3}&\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,{\text{1}}\hskip-0.56905pt_{3}-{{\boldsymbol{q}}}{\raisebox{0.51663pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\end{pmatrix}$}}&\text{\footnotesize{$\begin{pmatrix}{{\boldsymbol{0}}}&{{\boldsymbol{0}}}\\[3.0pt]
{{\boldsymbol{0}}}&{{\boldsymbol{0}}}\end{pmatrix}$}}\\[14.0pt]
\text{\footnotesize{$\begin{pmatrix}0&-\tfrac{1}{p}\big(\tfrac{2{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{p^{2}}(1-\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau})+\tfrac{1}{p}w\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\big){\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}^{{\scriptscriptstyle{\textsf{{T}}}}}\\[3.0pt]
0&-(u+\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{p^{2}})\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}^{{\scriptscriptstyle{\textsf{{T}}}}}\end{pmatrix}$}}&\text{\footnotesize{$\begin{pmatrix}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}&\tfrac{1}{p}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\\[3.0pt]

p\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,{\text{1}}\hskip-0.56905pt_{3}&\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,{\text{1}}\hskip-0.56905pt_{3}+{{\boldsymbol{q}}}{\raisebox{0.51663pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\end{pmatrix}$}}&\text{\footnotesize{$\begin{pmatrix}{{\boldsymbol{0}}}&{{\boldsymbol{0}}}\\[3.0pt]

p\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}&\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\end{pmatrix}$}}\end{pmatrix}$}}\right|_{{{\boldsymbol{x}}}={{\boldsymbol{x}}}_{\tau}}

Appx. C
ANOTHER APPROACH TO THE ORBIT ELEMENTS

The following does not, for the most part, yield anything new beyond what was given in section
2
. In particular, the result is the same as Eq.(
2.2
) and Eq.(
2.2
) (though the approach is a bit different). There is quite a bit of redundancy between the following and section
2
.
We include it here mostly for comparison with previous work by the authors in
[
12
,
14
]
(where something similar to the following was presented) and with earlier work by Schumacher
[
5
]
.
Some of the following expressions for the element ODEs may differ slightly from section
2
, though they are indeed equivalent. We also give an alternative element set, and their governing equations, whish is not addressed in section
2
.

Notation
.

We have been using “
\simeq
” to indicate relations that have been simplified using the integrals of motion
q=1
and
\lambda={\hat{{{\boldsymbol{q}}}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{p}}}=0
. Below, we will make free use of these relations and stop using the symbol “
\simeq
”. Although this means
{\ell}=p
, we will often continue to write
{\ell}
to keep in mind that this is the specific angular momentum magnitude.

We now pick up where section
1.4
left off, with closed-form Kepler solutions in projective coordinates.
We will stick with
\tau
as our chosen evolution parameter, where
{\mathrm{d}}t=(r^{2}/{\ell}){\mathrm{d}}\tau=1/({\ell}u^{2}){\mathrm{d}}\tau
, making
\tau
the true anomaly up to an additive constant.
Everything that follows could be also carried out using
s
as the evolution parameter rather than
\tau
(where
{\mathrm{d}}t=r^{2}{\mathrm{d}}s=u^{\scalebox{0.7}{$-2$}}{\mathrm{d}}s
). However, the
\tau
-parameterized Kepler dynamics have unit natural frequency
1
, whereas the
s
-parameterized dynamics have natural frequency
{\ell}
which is generally
not
constant in the perturbed case and the simple relation
\tau={\ell}s
does
not
hold. We therefore choose to use
\tau
rather than
s
for simplicity (in particular, to simplify the variation of parameters procedure).

We now consider the perturbed Kepler problem, with
\tau
-parameterized ODEs for
{{\boldsymbol{x}}}=({{\boldsymbol{q}}},u,{{\boldsymbol{p}}},w)\simeq({\bar{{{\boldsymbol{q}}}}},\acute{{\bar{{{\boldsymbol{q}}}}}})
given as in Eq.(
1.4
). Simplifying them using
q=1
and
\lambda={\hat{{{\boldsymbol{q}}}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{p}}}=0
leads to:

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{llll}\mathring{{{\boldsymbol{q}}}}\,=\,-{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\star}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{q}}}&\!\!=\,\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}{{\boldsymbol{p}}}&,\\[4.0pt]
\mathring{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}\,=\,-{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\star}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{p}}}+\mathring{t}{{\boldsymbol{f}}}&\!\!=\,-{\ell}{{\boldsymbol{q}}}+\mathring{t}{{\boldsymbol{f}}}&,\end{array}\hskip 9.24994pt\begin{array}[]{llll}\mathring{u}\,=\,w/{\ell}\\[4.0pt]
\mathring{w}\,=\,-{\ell}u+\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}+\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}f_{\scalebox{0.7}{$u$}}\end{array}

where
\mathring{t}=\tfrac{1}{{\ell}u^{2}}
, and where the generalized perturbation terms
{{\boldsymbol{f}}}={{\boldsymbol{F}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}\tfrac{\uppartial{{\boldsymbol{r}}}}{\uppartial{{\boldsymbol{q}}}}
and
f_{\scalebox{0.7}{$u$}}={{\boldsymbol{F}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}\tfrac{\uppartial{{\boldsymbol{r}}}}{\uppartial u}
were given in Eq.(
1.1
).

C.1  Elements from Simplified Kepler Solutions

For the above perturbed Kepler dynamics, we seek a solution that has the
same algebraic form
as the unperturbed Kepler solution in Eq.(
1.4
) or, equivalently, Eq.(
B.2
). In order to do this, we no longer treat
{{\boldsymbol{x}}}_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}
as a set of constants/initial conditions but, instead, as a set of slowly evolving/osculating elements,
{{\boldsymbol{x}}}_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}(\tau)
.
More precisely, we introduce a set of eight elements
{{\boldsymbol{\xi}}}:=({{\boldsymbol{Q}}},U,{{\boldsymbol{P}}},W)
, defined by a

\tau
-dependent coordinate transformation
{{\boldsymbol{x}}}\leftrightarrow{{\boldsymbol{\xi}}}
using the same (simplified) Kepler solution flow
\phi_{\tau}:{\mathbb{R}}^{8}\to{\mathbb{R}}^{8}
from Eq.(
B.2
)-Eq.(
B.2
):

\displaystyle{{\boldsymbol{x}}}\,=\,\phi_{\tau}({{\boldsymbol{\xi}}})\,=\,\Phi_{\tau}({\ell}){\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{\xi}}}\,+\,{{\boldsymbol{{\sigma}}}}_{\tau}({\ell})\hskip 18.49988pt\leftrightarrow\hskip 18.49988pt{{\boldsymbol{\xi}}}:=\,\phi_{\tau}^{\scriptscriptstyle{-\!1}}({{\boldsymbol{x}}})\,=\,\Phi_{-\tau}({\ell}){\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{x}}}\,+\,{{\boldsymbol{{\sigma}}}}_{-\tau}({\ell})

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{lllllll}{{\boldsymbol{q}}}\,=\,{{\boldsymbol{Q}}}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}+\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}{{\boldsymbol{P}}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\\[4.0pt]
{{\boldsymbol{p}}}\,=\,-{\ell}{{\boldsymbol{Q}}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}+{{\boldsymbol{P}}}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\\[4.0pt]
u\,=\,(U-\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}})\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,+\,\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}W\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,+\,\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}}\\[4.0pt]
w\,=\,-{\ell}(U-\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}})\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,+\,W\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\end{array}\hskip 18.49988pt\leftrightarrow\hskip 18.49988pt\begin{array}[]{lll}{{\boldsymbol{Q}}}:=\,{{\boldsymbol{q}}}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}-\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}{{\boldsymbol{p}}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\\[4.0pt]
{{\boldsymbol{P}}}:=\,{\ell}{{\boldsymbol{q}}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}+{{\boldsymbol{p}}}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\\[4.0pt]
U:=\,(u-\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}})\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}-\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}w\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}+\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}}\\[4.0pt]
W:=\,{\ell}(u-\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}})\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}+w\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\end{array}

It can be verified from the above, along with Eq.(
1.1
), that
{{\boldsymbol{Q}}}
,
{{\boldsymbol{P}}}
, and
{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}
satisfy the same relations as
{{\boldsymbol{q}}}
,
{{\boldsymbol{p}}}
, and
{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}
:

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{ll}{{\boldsymbol{Q}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{Q}}}\,=\,{{\boldsymbol{q}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{q}}}\,=\,1\\[4.0pt]
{{\boldsymbol{Q}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{P}}}\,=\,{{\boldsymbol{q}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{p}}}\,=\,0\\[4.0pt]
{{\boldsymbol{P}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{P}}}\,=\,{{\boldsymbol{p}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{p}}}\,=\,{\ell}^{2}\end{array}

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{ll}{{\boldsymbol{Q}}}\raisebox{0.58122pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\wedge\,}$}{{\boldsymbol{P}}}\,=\,{{\boldsymbol{q}}}\raisebox{0.58122pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\wedge\,}$}{{\boldsymbol{p}}}\,=\,{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\star}}\\[4.0pt]
{{\boldsymbol{Q}}}\raisebox{0.38751pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\times\,}$}{{\boldsymbol{P}}}\,=\,{{\boldsymbol{q}}}\raisebox{0.38751pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\times\,}$}{{\boldsymbol{p}}}\,=\,{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}\\[4.0pt]
{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{P}}}}}\raisebox{0.38751pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\times\,}$}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}\,=\,{{\boldsymbol{Q}}}\\[4.0pt]
{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}\raisebox{0.38751pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\times\,}$}{{\boldsymbol{Q}}}\,=\,{{\boldsymbol{P}}}\end{array}

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{lll}{\ell}^{2}=q^{2}p^{2}-({{\boldsymbol{q}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{p}}})^{2}\,=\,Q^{2}P^{2}-({{\boldsymbol{Q}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{P}}})^{2}\hskip 9.24994pt=p^{2}=P^{2}\\[4.0pt]
{\text{1}}\hskip-0.56905pt_{3}={\hat{{{\boldsymbol{q}}}}}{\raisebox{0.58122pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{q}}}}}+{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}{\raisebox{0.58122pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}+{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}{\raisebox{0.58122pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}\,=\,{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{Q}}}}}{\raisebox{0.58122pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{Q}}}}}+{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{P}}}}}{\raisebox{0.58122pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{P}}}}}+{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}{\raisebox{0.58122pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}\end{array}

Note in the transformation Eq.(
C.1
) that the
\tau
-dependent terms
\Phi_{\tau}
,
{{\boldsymbol{{\sigma}}}}_{\tau}
, and
{{\boldsymbol{{\varsigma}}}}_{\tau}
all also depend on
{\ell}
(no longer constant) and thus should be regarded as functions of
{{\boldsymbol{x}}}
in the transformation
{{\boldsymbol{\xi}}}=\phi_{\tau}^{\scriptscriptstyle{-\!1}}({{\boldsymbol{x}}})
, or as functions of
{{\boldsymbol{\xi}}}
in the transformation
{{\boldsymbol{x}}}=\phi_{\tau}({{\boldsymbol{\xi}}})
. More specifically, these terms depend on
{\ell}={\ell}({{\boldsymbol{q}}},{{\boldsymbol{p}}})={\ell}({{\boldsymbol{Q}}},{{\boldsymbol{P}}})
as given in Eq.(
C.1
), which simplifies to
{\ell}=p=P
. To clarify, we could write Eq.(
C.1
) more explicitly as:

\displaystyle\boxed{\begin{array}[]{lllllll}{{\boldsymbol{q}}}\,=\,{{\boldsymbol{Q}}}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}+\tfrac{1}{P}{{\boldsymbol{P}}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\\[3.0pt]
{{\boldsymbol{p}}}\,=\,-P{{\boldsymbol{Q}}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}+{{\boldsymbol{P}}}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\\[3.0pt]
u\,=\,(U-\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{P^{2}})\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,+\,\tfrac{1}{P}W\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,+\,\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{P^{2}}\\[3.0pt]
w\,=\,-P(U-\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{P^{2}})\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,+\,W\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\end{array}\hskip 18.49988pt\leftrightarrow\hskip 18.49988pt\begin{array}[]{lll}{{\boldsymbol{Q}}}:=\,{{\boldsymbol{q}}}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}-\tfrac{1}{p}{{\boldsymbol{p}}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\\[3.0pt]
{{\boldsymbol{P}}}:=\,p{{\boldsymbol{q}}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}+{{\boldsymbol{p}}}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\\[3.0pt]
U:=\,(u-\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{p^{2}})\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}-\tfrac{1}{p}w\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}+\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{p^{2}}\\[3.0pt]
W:=\,p(u-\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{p^{2}})\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}+w\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\end{array}}

By way of Eq.(
•
‣
1.4
) and Eq.(
C.1
), we also have the cartesian coordinates
({{\boldsymbol{r}}},{{\boldsymbol{{\nu}}}})
are given in terms of the elements by:

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{llllll}{{\boldsymbol{r}}}\,=\,\tfrac{1}{u}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{q}}}}}&\!\!\simeq\,\tfrac{1}{u}{{\boldsymbol{q}}}&=\,\dfrac{{{\boldsymbol{Q}}}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,+\,\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}{{\boldsymbol{P}}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}}{(U-\frac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}})\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,-\,W_{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,+\,\frac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}}}\\[14.0pt]
{{\boldsymbol{{\nu}}}}\,=\,-u({{\boldsymbol{q}}}\raisebox{0.58122pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\wedge\,}$}{{\boldsymbol{p}}}){\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{q}}}}}-w{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{q}}}}}&\!\!\simeq\,u{{\boldsymbol{p}}}-w{{\boldsymbol{q}}}&=\,U{{\boldsymbol{P}}}-W{{\boldsymbol{Q}}}+\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}}\big[{{\boldsymbol{P}}}(\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}-1)-{\ell}{{\boldsymbol{Q}}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\big]\end{array}

with
{\ell}^{2}\simeq p^{2}\simeq P^{2}
as in E.(
C.1
)

\,\scriptscriptstyle{\blacksquare}\,

The orbit elements defined above are not action-angle coordinates obtained from the Hamilton-Jacobi equation, nor are they any other type of canonical/symplectic coordinates.

Interpretation of the Elements.

Eq.(
C.1
) defines the elements
{{\boldsymbol{\xi}}}:=({{\boldsymbol{Q}}},U,{{\boldsymbol{P}}},W)
as a new set of “slow” coordinates in such a way that, in the perturbed case, the solutions for the “fast” coordinates,
{{\boldsymbol{x}}}_{\tau}
, are given in terms of the solutions
{{\boldsymbol{\xi}}}_{\tau}
by the same relation as they are given in terms of
{{\boldsymbol{x}}}_{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}
in the unperturbed case:

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{cc}{\text{\scriptsize{perturbed solution:}}}\\[3.0pt]
{{\boldsymbol{x}}}_{\tau}\,=\,\phi_{\tau}({{\boldsymbol{\xi}}}_{\tau})\end{array}

,

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{cc}{{\text{\scriptsize{unperturbed solution:}}}}\\[3.0pt]
{{\boldsymbol{x}}}_{\tau}\,=\,\phi_{\tau}({{\boldsymbol{\xi}}}_{\tau})\,=\,\phi_{\tau}({{\boldsymbol{x}}}_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}})\;\;,\;\;{{\boldsymbol{\xi}}}_{\tau}={{\boldsymbol{x}}}_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}\end{array}

where
\phi_{\tau}:{\mathbb{R}}^{8}\to{\mathbb{R}}^{8}
is the same map in both cases (the flow for the unperturbed Kepler dynamics).
Notably, in the unperturbed Kepler case, then
{{\boldsymbol{\xi}}}
has trivial solution
{{\boldsymbol{\xi}}}_{\tau}={{\boldsymbol{x}}}_{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}
, coinciding with the constant initial conditions.
We have
required
the perturbed solution satisfy the algebraic relations seen in Eq.(
C.1
).
Only for unperturbed Keplerian motion does it hold that
{{\boldsymbol{\xi}}}_{\tau}={{\boldsymbol{x}}}_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}
are constants coinciding with initial conditions.
For general perturbed motion,
{{\boldsymbol{\xi}}}
is now interpreted as a new set of “slow” coordinates that are
defined
in terms of the “fast” coordinates,
{{\boldsymbol{x}}}
, by Eq.(
C.1
).

Equations of Motion for the Elements
{{\boldsymbol{\xi}}}
.

Using variation of parameters, the governing equations of motion for the elements
{{\boldsymbol{\xi}}}=({{\boldsymbol{Q}}},U,{{\boldsymbol{P}}},W)
are found to be as follows (this will be derived shortly):

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{rllll}\mathring{{{\boldsymbol{Q}}}}&\!\!\!=\,-\tfrac{\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}}{{\ell}^{2}u^{2}}({\text{1}}\hskip-0.56905pt_{3}-{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}{\raisebox{0.58122pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}){\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{f}}}&=\,-\tfrac{\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}}{{\ell}^{2}u^{2}}({\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}{\raisebox{0.58122pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}){\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{f}}}\\[8.0pt]
\mathring{{{\boldsymbol{P}}}}&\!\!\!=\,\tfrac{1}{{\ell}u^{2}}\Big({\hat{{{\boldsymbol{P}}}}}{\raisebox{0.58122pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}\,+\,\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}({\text{1}}\hskip-0.56905pt_{3}-{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}{\raisebox{0.58122pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}})\Big){\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{f}}}&=\,\tfrac{1}{{\ell}u^{2}}\Big({\hat{{{\boldsymbol{P}}}}}{\raisebox{0.58122pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}\,+\,\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}{\raisebox{0.58122pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}\Big){\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{f}}}\\[8.0pt]
\mathring{U}&\!\!\!=\,-\tfrac{1}{{\ell}^{2}u^{2}}\Big(u^{2}f_{\scalebox{0.7}{$u$}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,+\,\big[U+\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}}-(u+\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}})\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\big]{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{f}}}\Big)\\[8.0pt]
\mathring{W}&\!\!\!=\,\tfrac{1}{{\ell}u^{2}}\Big(u^{2}f_{\scalebox{0.7}{$u$}}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,+\,(u+\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}})\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{f}}}\Big)\end{array}

where
{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{P}}}}}=\tfrac{1}{P}{{\boldsymbol{P}}}=\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}{{\boldsymbol{P}}}
.
It may be more useful to express the right-hand-side of the above using the cartesian components of the perturbing forces,
{{\boldsymbol{F}}}=-\tfrac{\uppartial V^{1}}{\uppartial{{\boldsymbol{r}}}}+{{\boldsymbol{a}}}^{{\scriptscriptstyle{{\mathrm{nc}}}}}
. To do so, we may substitute
{{\boldsymbol{f}}}={{\boldsymbol{F}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}\tfrac{\uppartial{{\boldsymbol{r}}}}{\uppartial{{\boldsymbol{q}}}}
and
f_{\scalebox{0.7}{$u$}}={{\boldsymbol{F}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}\tfrac{\uppartial{{\boldsymbol{r}}}}{\uppartial u}
from Eq.(
1.1
) and use:

\displaystyle{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{q}}}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{f}}}=0\hskip 18.49988pt,\hskip 18.49988pt{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{f}}}=\tfrac{1}{up}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{q}}}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\star}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{F}}}=\tfrac{1}{u}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{F}}}\hskip 18.49988pt,\hskip 18.49988pt{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{f}}}=\tfrac{1}{u}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{F}}}\hskip 18.49988pt,\hskip 18.49988ptf_{\scalebox{0.7}{$u$}}=-\tfrac{1}{u^{2}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{q}}}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{F}}}

(535)

where some of the above have used
q=1
and
{{\boldsymbol{q}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{p}}}=0
.
Equation Eq.(
C.1
) then leads
to:
41
41

41

The equations for
(U,W)
are equivalently written as:

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{lllllll}\mathring{U}\,=\,\tfrac{1}{{\ell}^{2}u^{3}}\Big((u{{\boldsymbol{q}}}+\tfrac{w}{{\ell}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}})\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,-\,\tfrac{2{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}}(1-\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}){\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}\Big){\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{F}}}&=\,\tfrac{1}{{\ell}^{2}u^{3}}\Big(u\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,{{\boldsymbol{q}}}\,-\,\big[\tfrac{2{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}}(1-\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau})-\tfrac{w}{{\ell}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\big]{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}\Big){\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{F}}}\\[4.0pt]
\mathring{W}\,=\,-\tfrac{1}{{\ell}u^{3}}\Big((u{{\boldsymbol{q}}}+\tfrac{w}{{\ell}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}})\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,-\,(\tfrac{W}{{\ell}}+\tfrac{2{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}){\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}\big){\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{F}}}&=\,-\tfrac{1}{{\ell}u^{3}}\Big(u\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,{{\boldsymbol{q}}}\,-\,\big[\tfrac{W}{{\ell}}-\tfrac{w}{{\ell}}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}+\tfrac{2{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\big]{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}\Big){\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{F}}}\end{array}

\displaystyle\boxed{\begin{array}[]{rlllll}\mathring{{{\boldsymbol{Q}}}}&\!\!\!=\,-\tfrac{\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}}{{\ell}^{2}u^{3}}({\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}{\raisebox{0.58122pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}){\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{F}}}&=\,-\tfrac{\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}}{{\ell}^{2}u^{3}}F_{{\ell}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}\\[8.0pt]
\mathring{{{\boldsymbol{P}}}}&\!\!\!=\,\tfrac{1}{{\ell}u^{3}}\big({\hat{{{\boldsymbol{P}}}}}{\raisebox{0.58122pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}\,+\,\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}{\raisebox{0.58122pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}\big){\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{F}}}&=\,\tfrac{1}{{\ell}u^{3}}\big(F_{\tau}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{P}}}}}\,+\,F_{{\ell}}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}\big)\\[8.0pt]
\mathring{U}&\!\!\!=\,\tfrac{1}{{\ell}^{2}u^{3}}\Big(u\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,{{\boldsymbol{q}}}\,-\,\big[U+\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}}-(u+\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}})\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\big]{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}\Big){\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{F}}}&=\,\tfrac{1}{{\ell}^{2}u^{3}}\Big(F_{r}u\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,-\,F_{\tau}\big[U+\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}}-(u+\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}})\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\big]\Big)\\[8.0pt]
\mathring{W}&\!\!\!=\,-\tfrac{1}{{\ell}u^{3}}\Big(u\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,{{\boldsymbol{q}}}\,-\,(u+\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}})\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}\Big){\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{F}}}&=\,-\tfrac{1}{{\ell}u^{3}}\Big(F_{r}u\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,-\,F_{\tau}(u+\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}})\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\Big)\end{array}}

where the second equalities follow from the fact that
\{{{\boldsymbol{q}}},{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}},{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}\}=\{{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{t}}}}}_{r},{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{t}}}}}_{\tau},{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{t}}}}}_{{\ell}}\}

is the LVLH basis (rather, its inertial cartesian components)
and where we have denoted by
(F_{r},F_{\tau},F_{{\ell}})
the LVLH components of the total perturbing force.
42
42

42

The force components
(F_{r},F_{\tau},F_{{\ell}})
in Eq.(
C.1
) are the components of the total perturbing force in the LVLH basis
\{{{\boldsymbol{q}}},{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}},{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}\}=\{{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{t}}}}}_{r},{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{t}}}}}_{\tau},{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{t}}}}}_{{\ell}}\}
. They are clearly
not
the inertial cartesian components which we denote by
{{\boldsymbol{F}}}:=-\tfrac{\uppartial V^{1}}{\uppartial{{\boldsymbol{r}}}}+{{\boldsymbol{a}}}^{{\scriptscriptstyle{{\mathrm{nc}}}}}=(F_{r_{1}},F_{r_{2}},F_{r_{3}})
.

\,\scriptscriptstyle{\blacksquare}\,

Note from Eq.(
C.1
) or Eq.(
C.1
) that if the perturbing force is also some central-force of any kind (i.e., if
{{\boldsymbol{F}}}=F_{r}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{r}}}}}=F_{r}{{\boldsymbol{q}}}
), then
\mathring{{{\boldsymbol{Q}}}}=\mathring{{{\boldsymbol{P}}}}=0
and these elements remain constant. Similarly, if
{{\boldsymbol{F}}}=F_{{\ell}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}
is only in the orbit normal direction, then
\mathring{U}=\mathring{W}=0
.

Although we have expressed the right-hand-side of the element’s governing equations using a mix of the “fast” coordinates
{{\boldsymbol{x}}}=({{\boldsymbol{q}}},u,{{\boldsymbol{p}}},w)
and the “slow” elements
{{\boldsymbol{\xi}}}=({{\boldsymbol{Q}}},U,{{\boldsymbol{P}}},W)
, these equations can be expressed entirely in terms of the elements themselves using the relations in Eq.(
C.1
) and Eq.(
C.1
).
For instance:

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{rclllllll}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}\;=&\!\!{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{q}}}}}\raisebox{0.38751pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\times\,}$}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}&=&{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{Q}}}}}\raisebox{0.38751pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\times\,}$}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{P}}}}}\\[3.0pt]
{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}{\raisebox{0.58122pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}\;=&\!\!{\text{1}}\hskip-0.56905pt_{3}-{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{q}}}}}{\raisebox{0.58122pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{q}}}}}-{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}{\raisebox{0.58122pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}&=&{\text{1}}\hskip-0.56905pt_{3}-{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{Q}}}}}{\raisebox{0.58122pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{Q}}}}}-{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{P}}}}}{\raisebox{0.58122pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{P}}}}}\\[3.0pt]
{\ell}^{2}\;=&\!\!q^{2}p^{2}-({{\boldsymbol{q}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{p}}})^{2}=p^{2}&=&Q^{2}P^{2}-({{\boldsymbol{Q}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{P}}})^{2}=P^{2}\end{array}

\displaystyle u{{\boldsymbol{q}}}+\tfrac{w}{{\ell}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}\,=\,U{{\boldsymbol{Q}}}+\tfrac{W}{{\ell}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{P}}}}}+\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}}({{\boldsymbol{q}}}-{{\boldsymbol{Q}}})\;=\;(U-\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}}){{\boldsymbol{Q}}}+\tfrac{W}{{\ell}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{P}}}}}+\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}}{{\boldsymbol{q}}}\hskip 9.24994pt=\;\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}}({{\boldsymbol{e}}}+{{\boldsymbol{q}}})

where, in the last line,
{{\boldsymbol{e}}}
is the eccentricity (see Eq.(
1.4
)).

Derivation of Eq.(
C.1
).

To obtain the governing equations of motion for the elements
{{\boldsymbol{\xi}}}
, we use the fact that the Kepler solution form — given by Eq.(
C.1
) — but with
{{\boldsymbol{\xi}}}
no longer constant, must satisfy the
perturbed
equations of motion for
{{\boldsymbol{x}}}
given by Eq.(
C
).
Differentiating Eq.(
C.1
) with respect to
\tau
yields:

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{rl}\mathring{{{\boldsymbol{q}}}}&=\big[-{{\boldsymbol{Q}}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}+\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}{{\boldsymbol{P}}}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\big]+\mathring{{{\boldsymbol{Q}}}}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}+\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}(\mathring{{{\boldsymbol{P}}}}-{{\boldsymbol{P}}}\tfrac{\mathring{{\ell}}}{{\ell}})\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\hskip 8.50012pt=\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}{{\boldsymbol{p}}}+\mathring{{{\boldsymbol{Q}}}}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}+\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}(\mathring{{{\boldsymbol{P}}}}-{{\boldsymbol{P}}}\tfrac{\mathring{{\ell}}}{{\ell}})\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\\[8.0pt]
\mathring{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}&=-\big[{\ell}{{\boldsymbol{Q}}}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}+{{\boldsymbol{P}}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\big]-({\ell}\mathring{{{\boldsymbol{Q}}}}+\mathring{{\ell}}{{\boldsymbol{Q}}})\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}+\mathring{{{\boldsymbol{P}}}}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\hskip 8.50012pt\;\;\;=-{\ell}{{\boldsymbol{q}}}-({\ell}\mathring{{{\boldsymbol{Q}}}}+\mathring{{\ell}}{{\boldsymbol{Q}}})\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}+\mathring{{{\boldsymbol{P}}}}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\\[8.0pt]
\mathring{u}&=\big[-(U-\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}})\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}+\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}W\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\big]+(\mathring{U}+\tfrac{2{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{3}}\mathring{{\ell}})\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}+\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}(\mathring{W}-\tfrac{\mathring{{\ell}}}{{\ell}}W)\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}-\tfrac{2{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{3}}\mathring{{\ell}}\\[3.0pt]
&=\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}w+(\mathring{U}+\tfrac{2{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{3}}\mathring{{\ell}})\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}+\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}(\mathring{W}-\tfrac{\mathring{{\ell}}}{{\ell}}W)\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}-\tfrac{2{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{3}}\mathring{{\ell}}\\[8.0pt]
\mathring{w}&=-\big[{\ell}(U-\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}})\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}+W\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\big]-{\ell}(\mathring{U}+\tfrac{2{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{3}}\mathring{{\ell}})\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}-\mathring{{\ell}}(U-\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}})\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}+\mathring{W}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\\[3.0pt]
&=-{\ell}(u-\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}})-{\ell}(\mathring{U}+\tfrac{2{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{3}}\mathring{{\ell}})\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}-\mathring{{\ell}}(U-\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}})\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}+\mathring{W}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\end{array}

where the second equality for each of the above follows from the requirement that the algebraic relations in Eq.(
C.1
) must still hold.
Equating the above to the perturbed equations of motion given in Eq.(
C
), we find

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathring{{{\boldsymbol{q}}}}=\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}{{\boldsymbol{p}}}\\[4.0pt]
\mathring{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}+{\ell}{{\boldsymbol{q}}}=\tfrac{1}{{\ell}u^{2}}{{\boldsymbol{f}}}\\[4.0pt]
\mathring{u}=\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}w\\[4.0pt]
\mathring{w}+{\ell}u-\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}}=\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}f_{\scalebox{0.7}{$u$}}\end{array}

\displaystyle\Rightarrow

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathring{{{\boldsymbol{Q}}}}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}+\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}(\mathring{{{\boldsymbol{P}}}}-{{\boldsymbol{P}}}\tfrac{\mathring{{\ell}}}{{\ell}})\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}={{\boldsymbol{0}}}\\[4.0pt]
-({\ell}\mathring{{{\boldsymbol{Q}}}}+\mathring{{\ell}}{{\boldsymbol{Q}}})\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}+\mathring{{{\boldsymbol{P}}}}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,=\,\tfrac{1}{{\ell}u^{2}}{{\boldsymbol{f}}}\\[4.0pt]
(\mathring{U}+\tfrac{2{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{3}}\mathring{{\ell}})\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}+\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}(\mathring{W}-\tfrac{\mathring{{\ell}}}{{\ell}}W)\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}-\tfrac{2{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{3}}\mathring{{\ell}}=0\\[4.0pt]
-{\ell}(\mathring{U}+\tfrac{2{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{3}}\mathring{{\ell}})\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}-\mathring{{\ell}}(U-\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}})\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}+\mathring{W}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}=\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}f_{\scalebox{0.7}{$u$}}\end{array}

The first two relations may be solved for
\mathring{{{\boldsymbol{Q}}}}
and
\mathring{{{\boldsymbol{P}}}}
, and the last two relations for
\mathring{U}
and
\mathring{W}
, leading to:

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{llll}\mathring{{{\boldsymbol{Q}}}}\,=\,-\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}(\tfrac{1}{{\ell}u^{2}}{{\boldsymbol{f}}}-\tfrac{\mathring{{\ell}}}{{\ell}}{{\boldsymbol{p}}})\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\\[4.0pt]
\mathring{{{\boldsymbol{P}}}}\,=\,(\tfrac{1}{{\ell}u^{2}}{{\boldsymbol{f}}}-\tfrac{\mathring{{\ell}}}{{\ell}}{{\boldsymbol{p}}})\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,+\,\tfrac{\mathring{{\ell}}}{{\ell}}{{\boldsymbol{P}}}\end{array}\hskip 8.50012pt,\hskip 8.50012pt\begin{array}[]{llll}\mathring{U}\,=\,-\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}(\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}f_{\scalebox{0.7}{$u$}}\,-\,\tfrac{\mathring{{\ell}}}{{\ell}}w)\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}-\tfrac{2{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{3}}\mathring{{\ell}}(1-\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau})\\[4.0pt]
\mathring{W}\,=\,(\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}f_{\scalebox{0.7}{$u$}}-\tfrac{\mathring{{\ell}}}{{\ell}}w)\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,+\,\tfrac{\mathring{{\ell}}}{{\ell}}W\,+\,\tfrac{2{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}}\mathring{{\ell}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\end{array}

,

\displaystyle\mathring{{\ell}}=\mathring{p}=\tfrac{1}{{\ell}u^{2}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{f}}}=\tfrac{1}{{\ell}u^{3}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{F}}}

After substitution of
\mathring{{\ell}}
as given above (from Eq.(
1.2
)), and rearranging some terms in the ODEs for
(U,W)
, we obtain:

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{rllll}\mathring{{{\boldsymbol{Q}}}}&\!\!\!=\,-\tfrac{\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}}{{\ell}^{2}u^{2}}\big({\text{1}}\hskip-0.56905pt_{3}-{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}{\raisebox{0.51663pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}\big){\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{f}}}\\[4.0pt]
\mathring{{{\boldsymbol{P}}}}&\!\!\!=\,\tfrac{1}{{\ell}u^{2}}\Big(\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}({\text{1}}\hskip-0.56905pt_{3}-{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}{\raisebox{0.51663pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}})+{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{P}}}}}{\raisebox{0.51663pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}\Big){\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{f}}}\end{array}\hskip 8.50012pt,\hskip 8.50012pt\begin{array}[]{rllll}\mathring{U}&\!\!\!=\,-\tfrac{1}{{\ell}^{2}u^{2}}\Big((u^{2}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau})f_{\scalebox{0.7}{$u$}}\,+\,\big[\tfrac{2{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}}(1-\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau})-\tfrac{w}{{\ell}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\big]{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{f}}}\Big)\\[4.0pt]
\mathring{W}&\!\!\!=\,\tfrac{1}{{\ell}u^{2}}\Big((u^{2}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau})f_{\scalebox{0.7}{$u$}}\,+\,\big[\tfrac{W}{{\ell}}-\tfrac{w}{{\ell}}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}+\tfrac{2{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\big]{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{f}}}\Big)\end{array}

The above is equivalent to the ODEs that were claimed in Eq.(
C.1
). This equivalence is seen once one makes use of the following relations:

\displaystyle({\text{1}}\hskip-0.56905pt_{3}-{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}{\raisebox{0.51663pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}){\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{f}}}\,=\,({\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}{\raisebox{0.51663pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}){\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{f}}}\hskip 17.00024pt,\hskip 17.00024pt\begin{array}[]{lllll}\tfrac{2{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}}(1-\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau})-\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}w\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\;=\;U+\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}}-(u+\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}})\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\\[4.0pt]
\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}W-\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}w\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}+\tfrac{2{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\;=\;(u+\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}})\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\end{array}

The first of the above is explained in the footnote.
43
43

43

We have indicated that
({\text{1}}\hskip-0.56905pt_{3}-{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}{\raisebox{0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}){\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{f}}}=({\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}{\raisebox{0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}){\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{f}}}
. This is verified as follows. It was shown previously that
{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{q}}}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{f}}}=0
always holds (see Eq.(
1.1
)). Also, recall that
\{{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{q}}}}}={{\boldsymbol{q}}},{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}},{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}\}=\{{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{t}}}}}_{r},{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{t}}}}}_{\tau},{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{t}}}}}_{{\ell}}\}
are the inertial cartesian components of the LVLH basis such that
{\text{1}}\hskip-0.56905pt_{3}={\hat{{{\boldsymbol{q}}}}}{\raisebox{0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{q}}}}}+{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}{\raisebox{0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}+{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}{\raisebox{0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}
. Therefore:

\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad({\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}{\raisebox{0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}){\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{f}}}\,=\,({\text{1}}\hskip-0.56905pt_{3}-{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{q}}}}}{\raisebox{0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{q}}}}}-{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}{\raisebox{0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}){\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{f}}}\,=\,({\text{1}}\hskip-0.56905pt_{3}-{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}{\raisebox{0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}){\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{f}}}
.

C.2  Alternative Elements

Our element set
{{\boldsymbol{\xi}}}=({{\boldsymbol{Q}}},U,{{\boldsymbol{P}}},W)
was defined from the coordinates
{{\boldsymbol{x}}}=({{\boldsymbol{q}}},u,{{\boldsymbol{p}}},w)

using the unperturbed
\tau
-parameterized Kepler flow.
However, one could, if they wish, make any number of adjustments to these elements (so long as they still fully define the state
({{\boldsymbol{r}}},{{\boldsymbol{{\nu}}}})
). Here, we will briefly present an alternative choice for the “
W
element” and an alternative choice for the “
U
element”. But, first, we address what would seem to be an intuitive alternative element set, but which turns out to be flawed.

An
Incomplete
Element Set.

Given that the preceding developments used
\tau
, not
s
, as the evolution parameter, it may seem odd that we used the state coordinates
({{\boldsymbol{q}}},u,{{\boldsymbol{p}}},w)=({\bar{{{\boldsymbol{q}}}}},\tfrac{\mathrm{d}{\bar{{{\boldsymbol{q}}}}}}{\mathrm{d}s})
to define associated elements
({{\boldsymbol{Q}}},U,{{\boldsymbol{P}}},W)
and their
\tau
-parameterized governing equations. It would seem more fitting to instead use coordinates
({\bar{{{\boldsymbol{q}}}}},\tfrac{\mathrm{d}{\bar{{{\boldsymbol{q}}}}}}{\mathrm{d}\tau})=({{\boldsymbol{q}}},u,{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}},\tilde{w})
— where
{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}:={{\boldsymbol{p}}}/p={{\boldsymbol{p}}}/{\ell}=\mathring{{{\boldsymbol{q}}}}
and
\tilde{w}:=w/{\ell}=\tfrac{u^{2}}{{\ell}}p_{u}=\mathring{u}
— to define associated elements
({{\boldsymbol{Q}}},U,{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{P}}}}},\tilde{W})
, where
{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{P}}}}}:={{\boldsymbol{P}}}/P={{\boldsymbol{P}}}/{\ell}
and
\tilde{W}:=W/{\ell}
.
After going through the variation of parameters procedure to find the governing equations for
({{\boldsymbol{Q}}},U,{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{P}}}}},\tilde{W})
,
the result
is:
44
44

44

Deriving the governing equations of
({{\boldsymbol{Q}}},U,{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{P}}}}},\tilde{W})
mirrors the preceding developments for deriving those of
({{\boldsymbol{Q}}},U,{{\boldsymbol{P}}},W)
.
We would simply replace
w
and
W
with
w={\ell}\tilde{w}
and
W={\ell}\tilde{W}
and replace
{{\boldsymbol{p}}}
and
{{\boldsymbol{P}}}
with
{{\boldsymbol{p}}}={\ell}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}
and
{{\boldsymbol{P}}}={\ell}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{P}}}}}
, in Eq.(
C.1
). The equations of motion for the “fast” coordinates would simply be
\mathring{{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}}={\hbox to0.0pt{$\,\mathring{\vphantom{{{\boldsymbol{q}}}}}\mkern 4.5mu\mathring{\vphantom{{{\boldsymbol{q}}}}}$\hss}}{{\boldsymbol{q}}}
and
\mathring{\tilde{w}}={\hbox to0.0pt{$\,\mathring{\vphantom{u}}\mkern 4.5mu\mathring{\vphantom{u}}$\hss}}u
given by Eq.(
1.2
) or Eq.(
1.2
).

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{lllll}\mathring{{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{P}}}}}}\,=\,\tfrac{\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}}{{\ell}^{2}u^{3}}({\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}{\raisebox{0.58122pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{{\ell}}}}}}){\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{F}}}&\hskip 9.24994pt,\end{array}\hskip 18.49988pt\begin{array}[]{lllll}\mathring{\tilde{W}}\,=\,-\tfrac{1}{{\ell}^{2}u^{3}}\Big((u{{\boldsymbol{q}}}+\tilde{w}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}})\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,-\,\tfrac{2{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}\Big){\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{F}}}\end{array}

with
(\mathring{{{\boldsymbol{Q}}}},\mathring{U})
the same as in Eq.(
C.1
).
The elements
({{\boldsymbol{Q}}},U,{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{P}}}}},\tilde{W})
and their governing equations were also derived by Schumacher in
[
5
]
.
Although the above equations for
\mathring{{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{P}}}}}}
and
\mathring{\tilde{W}}
are slightly simpler than those for
\mathring{{{\boldsymbol{P}}}}
and
\mathring{W}
seen in equation Eq.(
C.1
), there is problem:
the set
({{\boldsymbol{Q}}},U,{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{P}}}}},\tilde{W})
is not a complete element set
.
The coordinates
({{\boldsymbol{q}}},u,{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}},\tilde{w})
do
not
fully define the state
({{\boldsymbol{r}}},{{\boldsymbol{{\nu}}}})
and, thus, neither does the set
({{\boldsymbol{Q}}},U,{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{P}}}}},\tilde{W})
. The issue lies in replacing
{{\boldsymbol{p}}}
with
{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}
and, likewise, replacing
{{\boldsymbol{P}}}
with
{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{P}}}}}
. For instance, there is no way to express the angular momentum in terms of
({{\boldsymbol{q}}},{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}})
or
({{\boldsymbol{Q}}},{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{P}}}}})
. If we wished to use
({{\boldsymbol{q}}},u,{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}},\tilde{w})
or
({{\boldsymbol{Q}}},U,{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{P}}}}},\tilde{W})
, then we would need to include the angular momentum
{\ell}
(or some equivalent) as a ninth element with
\mathring{{\ell}}
governed by Eq.(
C.1
). While there is nothing inherently wrong with adding
{\ell}
as a ninth element, it is unnecessary given the availability of the complete element set
({{\boldsymbol{Q}}},U,{{\boldsymbol{P}}},W)
.

Alternative
(U,W)
Elements.

One
could
exchange the pair
(U,W)
for some alternative
(\tilde{U},\tilde{W})
in order to slightly simply the transformation and the dynamics.
First, we note that the ODE for
(U,W)
in Eq.(
C.1
) are equivalently written as:

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{lllllll}\mathring{U}\,=\,\tfrac{1}{{\ell}^{2}u^{3}}\Big((u{{\boldsymbol{q}}}+\tfrac{w}{{\ell}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}})\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,-\,\tfrac{2{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}}(1-\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}){\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}\Big){\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{F}}}&=\,\tfrac{1}{{\ell}^{2}u^{3}}\Big(F_{r}u\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,-\,F_{\tau}\big[U+\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}}-(u+\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}})\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\big]\Big)\\[4.0pt]
\mathring{W}\,=\,-\tfrac{1}{{\ell}u^{3}}\Big((u{{\boldsymbol{q}}}+\tfrac{w}{{\ell}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}})\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,-\,(\tfrac{W}{{\ell}}+\tfrac{2{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}){\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}\big){\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{F}}}&=\,-\tfrac{1}{{\ell}u^{3}}\Big(F_{r}u\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,-\,F_{\tau}(u+\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}})\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\Big)\end{array}

An Alternative “
W
Element”.

While replacing
{{\boldsymbol{P}}}
with
{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{P}}}}}

with
\tilde{W}:=W/{\ell}
. That is, one could use
({{\boldsymbol{Q}}},U,{{\boldsymbol{P}}},\tilde{W})
as a valid set of orbit elements. The “
(u,w)

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{lll}\tilde{w}:=w/{\ell}\\[4.0pt]
\tilde{W}:=W/{\ell}\end{array}

,

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{lllllll}u\,=\,(U-\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}})\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,+\,\tilde{W}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,+\,\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}}\\[4.0pt]
\tilde{w}\,=\,-(U-\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}})\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,+\,\tilde{W}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\end{array}\hskip 9.24994pt\leftrightarrow\hskip 9.24994pt\begin{array}[]{lll}U:=\,(u-\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}})\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}-\tilde{w}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}+\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}}\\[4.0pt]
\tilde{W}:=\,(u-\tfrac{{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}})\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}+\tilde{w}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\end{array}

If using the element set
({{\boldsymbol{Q}}},U,{{\boldsymbol{P}}},\tilde{W})

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{lllll}\mathring{\tilde{W}}\,=\,-\tfrac{1}{{\ell}^{2}u^{3}}\Big((u{{\boldsymbol{q}}}+\tilde{w}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}})\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,-\,\tfrac{2{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}}\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}\Big){\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{F}}}\end{array}

An Alternative “

Looking at the transformation in Eq.(

for

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{lllll}\mathring{\tilde{U}}\,=\,\tfrac{1}{{\ell}^{2}u^{3}}\Big((u{{\boldsymbol{q}}}+\tfrac{w}{{\ell}}{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}})\sin{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,+\,\tfrac{2{\textnormal{{{k}}}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}}\cos{{\mkern-2.0mu}}{\tau}\,{\hat{{{\boldsymbol{p}}}}}\Big){\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{{\boldsymbol{F}}}\end{array}

where, for any of the above dynamics in Eq.(
